Greg's Global Submarine Cable Map

279 approximate cable-system networks joined to published stations

License, attribution, and modifications

  • License: GNU GPL, version unspecified
  • Attribution: Greg Mahlknecht / Greg's Global Submarine Cable Map.
  • Citation: Greg Mahlknecht, Greg's Global Submarine Cable Map, ArcGIS item 00ae4550cd9b41dd93d33972bb8520bb.
  • Modifications: CommunicationNetworkDatasets.jl normalized the source records into a simple undirected graph, normalized identifiers and units, and rendered this plot over a modified Natural Earth public-domain basemap.
  • Use and redistribution: Upstream names GNU GPL without a version; license scope and version are ambiguous and this is not an SPDX claim.
